= Static([CostChange { what: Objects { quant: NthEachTurn { n: 1, of: You }, filter: Filter { alts: [], kind: Some(Spell), posts: [XInManaCost], or: None } }, by: Mana(["1"]), less: true, per: Some(Objects { quant: Plural, filter: Filter { alts: [], kind: Some(Counter(Some("+1/+1"))), posts: [On(Objects { quant: SelfRef, filter: Filter { alts: [], kind: None, posts: [], or: None } })], or: None } }) }])
= Triggered { trigger: When(Casts { who: You, what: Objects { quant: NthEachTurn { n: 1, of: You }, filter: Filter { alts: [], kind: Some(Spell), posts: [XInManaCost], or: None } } }), effect: [PutCounters { n: N(2), kind: "+1/+1", on: Objects { quant: SelfRef, filter: Filter { alts: [], kind: None, posts: [], or: None } } }], limits: [] }

## Zimone, Quandrix Prodigy
face: Zimone, Quandrix Prodigy
type: Legendary Creature — Human Wizard
cost: {G}{U}
> {1}, {T}: You may put a land card from your hand onto the battlefield tapped.
> {4}, {T}: Draw a card. If you control eight or more lands, draw two cards instead.
= Activated { cost: [Mana(Mana(["1"])), Tap], effect: [May { who: You, then: [Move { what: Objects { quant: One, filter: Filter { alts: [[Type("land")]], kind: Some(Card), posts: [In(Hand(You))], or: None } }, from: None, to: Battlefield, tapped: true, transformed: false, under: None, order: None }] }], limits: [] }
= Activated { cost: [Mana(Mana(["4"])), Tap], effect: [Draw { who: You, n: N(1) }, Instead { cond: YouControl(Objects { quant: AtLeast(N(8)), filter: Filter { alts: [[Type("land")]], kind: None, posts: [], or: None } }), then: [Draw { who: You, n: N(2) }] }], limits: [] }
